<script>
/* ================= Direct PDF export + smart printing (v1.10.3) =================
   - PDF حقيقي: نصوص متجهة حقيقية (وليست صورة للصفحة) بخطوط مضمّنة، بنفس تخطيط صفحة الطباعة.
   - الطباعة: تعمل في المتصفحات مباشرة، وفي تطبيقات الأندرويد/الحاسوب عبر الجسر الاختياري window.DaragatNative
     (راجع README-NATIVE.md). عند عدم توفر الطباعة المباشرة يعرض التطبيق بديلاً واضحاً بدل الفشل الصامت.
   - الحفظ: نافذة اختيار الحفظ (سطح المكتب) / الجسر الأصلي / مشاركة / تنزيل / فتح — مع رسائل صادقة.
   Native bridge (اختياري) — window.DaragatNative:
       savePdf(base64, fileName)       -> يحفظ الملف ويعيد "ok" | "cancel" | "error"
       printHtml(html, optionsJson)    -> يطبع صفحة HTML كاملة (الأفضل)
       printPdf(base64, fileName)      -> بديل: يطبع ملف PDF
*/
(function(){
  'use strict';

  var PDF_BUSY = false;
  var PDF_REQUEST = null;
  var PDF_DECORATE_TIMER = null;
  var MM = 25.4 / 96;                 // 1 CSS px = MM millimetres
  var PX = 96 / 25.4;                 // 1 mm = PX CSS px

  var UA = String((typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && navigator.userAgent) || '');
  var IS_ANDROID_WV = /Android/i.test(UA) && /;\s*wv\)/i.test(UA);
  var IS_IOS_WV = /iPhone|iPad|iPod/i.test(UA) && !/Safari\//i.test(UA);
  var IS_WEBVIEW = IS_ANDROID_WV || IS_IOS_WV;

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- small helpers
  function pdfToast(message, error){
    var id = 'daragatPdfToast', el = document.getElementById(id);
    if(!el){
      el = document.createElement('div');
      el.id = id;
      el.setAttribute('role', 'status');
      el.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;z-index:2147483646;left:50%;bottom:24px;transform:translateX(-50%);max-width:min(92vw,460px);padding:12px 16px;border-radius:12px;background:#263238;color:#fff;box-shadow:0 8px 28px rgba(0,0,0,.28);font:700 13px Tahoma,Arial,sans-serif;text-align:center;line-height:1.6;direction:rtl;display:none;cursor:pointer';
      el.onclick = function(){ el.style.display = 'none'; };
      document.body.appendChild(el);
    }
    clearTimeout(el.__t);
    el.textContent = message || '';
    el.style.background = error ? '#b71c1c' : '#263238';
    el.style.display = message ? 'block' : 'none';
    if(message) el.__t = setTimeout(function(){ el.style.display = 'none'; }, error ? 9000 : 3200);
  }
  function pdfStamp(){
    var d = new Date(), p = function(n){ return String(n).padStart(2, '0'); };
    return d.getFullYear() + p(d.getMonth() + 1) + p(d.getDate()) + '-' + p(d.getHours()) + p(d.getMinutes());
  }
  function pdfSafeName(label){
    var s = String(label || 'طباعة').replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|\n\r]+/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    s = s.replace(/^(🖨️|📄)+/, '').trim() || 'طباعة';
    return 'Daragat-' + s + '-' + pdfStamp() + '.pdf';
  }
  function pdfEnsureExtension(name){
    var s = String(name || '').trim().replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|\n\r]+/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
    if(!s) s = 'Daragat-PDF-' + pdfStamp();
    return /\.pdf$/i.test(s) ? s : s + '.pdf';
  }
  function escAttr(s){ return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;'); }
  function sleep(ms){ return new Promise(function(r){ setTimeout(r, ms); }); }
  function blobToBase64(blob){
    return new Promise(function(res, rej){
      var r = new FileReader();
      r.onload = function(){ var s = String(r.result || ''), i = s.indexOf(','); res(i >= 0 ? s.slice(i + 1) : s); };
      r.onerror = function(){ rej(r.error || new Error('تعذرت قراءة الملف')); };
      r.readAsDataURL(blob);
    });
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- native bridge (Android / Desktop apps)
  function nativeApi(){
    try{ var n = window.DaragatNative; if(n && (typeof n === 'object' || typeof n === 'function')) return n; }catch(e){}
    return null;
  }
  function nativeHas(name){
    try{ var n = nativeApi(); return !!(n && typeof n[name] !== 'undefined' && n[name] !== null); }catch(e){ return false; }
  }
  function nativeOutcome(r){
    if(r === undefined || r === null || r === true) return 'ok';
    if(r === false) return 'error';
    if(typeof r === 'object'){
      if(r.cancelled || r.canceled || r.cancel) return 'cancel';
      if(r.ok === false || r.error) return 'error';
      return 'ok';
    }
    var s = String(r).trim().toLowerCase();
    if(!s || s === 'ok' || s === 'true' || s === 'success' || s === 'saved' || s === 'printed') return 'ok';
    if(/^cancel/.test(s)) return 'cancel';
    if(/^(false|error|fail)/.test(s)) return 'error';
    return 'ok';
  }
  async function nativeInvoke(name, a, b){
    var n = nativeApi();
    return await Promise.resolve(n[name](a, b));
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- modal helper
  function closeModal(id){ var m = document.getElementById(id); if(m) m.remove(); }
  function openModal(id, title, bodyHtml){
    closeModal(id);
    var modal = document.createElement('div');
    modal.id = id;
    modal.setAttribute('dir', 'rtl');
    modal.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:2147483647;background:rgba(0,0,0,.62);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:16px;direction:rtl;font-family:Tahoma,Arial,sans-serif';
    modal.innerHTML = '<div style="width:min(440px,100%);max-height:92vh;overflow:auto;background:#fff;border-radius:18px;padding:20px;box-shadow:0 18px 60px rgba(0,0,0,.35);color:#263238;text-align:right">' +
      '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;margin-bottom:10px"><h3 style="margin:0;color:#7a0e2e;font-size:18px">' + title + '</h3>' +
      '<button type="button" data-x="close" style="border:0;background:#f1f3f4;color:#263238;border-radius:9px;width:34px;height:34px;font-size:18px;cursor:pointer">✕</button></div>' +
      bodyHtml + '</div>';
    document.body.appendChild(modal);
    return modal;
  }
  var BTN = 'padding:13px;border:0;border-radius:10px;color:#fff;font-weight:900;font-family:inherit;font-size:14px;cursor:pointer;width:100%';

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- save chooser (used when no direct save path exists)
  function pdfSaveSupportsPicker(){ return typeof window.showSaveFilePicker === 'function'; }
  function pdfSaveSupportsShare(blob, name){
    try{
      var f = new File([blob], name, {type: 'application/pdf'});
      return !!(navigator.share && navigator.canShare && navigator.canShare({files: [f]}));
    }catch(e){ return false; }
  }
  function pdfCloseChooser(){ closeModal('daragatPdfSaveModal'); }

  function pdfCreateChooser(blob, suggestedName){
    return new Promise(function(resolve){
      var name = pdfEnsureExtension(suggestedName), acted = false, result = false, urls = [];
      var picker = pdfSaveSupportsPicker(), share = pdfSaveSupportsShare(blob, name);
      var body =
        '<p style="margin:0 0 12px;color:#607d8b;font-size:13px;line-height:1.7">الملف جاهز. اختر طريقة الحفظ المناسبة لجهازك.</p>' +
        '<label style="display:block;font-size:13px;font-weight:800;margin-bottom:6px">اسم الملف</label>' +
        '<input id="pdfSaveName" type="text" dir="ltr" value="' + escAttr(name) + '" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;padding:12px;border:2px solid #d4af37;border-radius:10px;font:700 14px Tahoma,Arial,sans-serif;color:#263238;background:#fff;margin-bottom:12px">' +
        '<div style="display:grid;gap:8px">' +
        (picker ? '<button type="button" id="pdfSavePicker" style="' + BTN + ';background:#7a0e2e">📁 اختيار المجلد والاسم</button>' : '') +
        (share ? '<button type="button" id="pdfSaveShare" style="' + BTN + ';background:#1565c0">📤 مشاركة / حفظ في الملفات</button>' : '') +
        '<button type="button" id="pdfSaveDownload" style="' + BTN + ';background:#2e7d32">⬇️ تنزيل الملف</button>' +
        '<button type="button" id="pdfSaveOpen" style="' + BTN + ';background:#6a1b9a">👁️ فتح الملف</button>' +
        '<button type="button" id="pdfSaveCancel" style="padding:10px;border:1px solid #cfd8dc;border-radius:10px;background:#fff;color:#546e7a;font-weight:800;font-family:inherit;cursor:pointer">إلغاء</button>' +
        '</div>' +
        '<div id="pdfSaveStatus" style="display:none;margin-top:12px;padding:10px 12px;border-radius:10px;font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.8"></div>' +
        (IS_WEBVIEW ? '<div style="margin-top:12px;padding:10px 12px;border-radius:10px;background:#fff8e1;color:#7a5b00;font-size:12px;line-height:1.8">ملاحظة: أنت تستخدم التطبيق داخل نافذة ويب مدمجة، وقد لا تدعم التنزيل المباشر. إن لم يظهر الملف بعد «تنزيل» فاستخدم «مشاركة»، أو افتح الموقع من متصفح Chrome.</div>' : '');
      var modal = openModal('daragatPdfSaveModal', 'حفظ ملف PDF', body);
      var input = modal.querySelector('#pdfSaveName');
      var statusEl = modal.querySelector('#pdfSaveStatus');
      var cancelBtn = modal.querySelector('#pdfSaveCancel');
      function currentName(){ return pdfEnsureExtension((input && input.value) || name); }
      function status(msg, kind){
        statusEl.style.display = 'block';
        statusEl.style.background = kind === 'err' ? '#ffebee' : (kind === 'ok' ? '#e8f5e9' : '#e3f2fd');
        statusEl.style.color = kind === 'err' ? '#b71c1c' : (kind === 'ok' ? '#1b5e20' : '#0d47a1');
        statusEl.textContent = msg;
      }
      function markDone(kind){ acted = true; if(kind) result = kind; else if(!result) result = 'handed'; cancelBtn.textContent = 'إغلاق'; }
      function finish(){
        pdfCloseChooser();
        setTimeout(function(){ urls.forEach(function(u){ try{ URL.revokeObjectURL(u); }catch(e){} }); }, 120000);
        resolve(acted ? result : false);
      }
      function blobUrl(){ var u = URL.createObjectURL(blob); urls.push(u); return u; }
      modal.querySelector('[data-x="close"]').onclick = finish;
      cancelBtn.onclick = finish;
      modal.addEventListener('click', function(e){ if(e.target === modal) finish(); });
      modal.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){ if(e.key === 'Escape') finish(); });

      var pickerBtn = modal.querySelector('#pdfSavePicker');
      if(pickerBtn) pickerBtn.onclick = async function(){
        try{
          var handle = await window.showSaveFilePicker({suggestedName: currentName(), types: [{description: 'PDF document', accept: {'application/pdf': ['.pdf']}}]});
          var w = await handle.createWritable(); await w.write(blob); await w.close();
          markDone('saved'); pdfToast('تم حفظ ملف PDF في المكان الذي اخترته.'); finish();
        }catch(e){ if(!(e && e.name === 'AbortError')) status('تعذر الحفظ بهذه الطريقة: ' + (e && e.message || e), 'err'); }
      };
      var shareBtn = modal.querySelector('#pdfSaveShare');
      if(shareBtn) shareBtn.onclick = async function(){
        try{
          var file = new File([blob], currentName(), {type: 'application/pdf'});
          await navigator.share({title: currentName(), files: [file]});
          markDone('handed'); pdfToast('تم تمرير الملف إلى نافذة المشاركة.'); finish();
        }catch(e){ if(!(e && e.name === 'AbortError')) status('تعذرت المشاركة: ' + (e && e.message || e), 'err'); }
      };
      modal.querySelector('#pdfSaveDownload').onclick = function(){
        try{
          var a = document.createElement('a');
          a.href = blobUrl(); a.download = currentName(); a.rel = 'noopener'; a.style.display = 'none';
          document.body.appendChild(a); a.click();
          setTimeout(function(){ try{ a.remove(); }catch(e){} }, 2000);
          markDone('handed');
          status('تم إرسال أمر التنزيل إلى النظام. ابحث عن الملف في مجلد «التنزيلات» (Downloads) أو من إشعار التنزيل. إن لم يظهر فجرّب «مشاركة» أو «فتح الملف».', 'info');
        }catch(e){ status('تعذر التنزيل: ' + (e && e.message || e), 'err'); }
      };
      modal.querySelector('#pdfSaveOpen').onclick = function(){
        try{
          var w = window.open(blobUrl(), '_blank');
          if(w){ markDone('handed'); status('تم فتح الملف في نافذة جديدة؛ يمكنك حفظه أو طباعته من هناك.', 'ok'); }
          else status('منع المتصفح فتح نافذة جديدة. استخدم «تنزيل الملف» أو «مشاركة».', 'err');
        }catch(e){ status('تعذر فتح الملف: ' + (e && e.message || e), 'err'); }
      };
      setTimeout(function(){ if(input && !/Android|iPhone|iPad/i.test(UA)){ input.focus(); input.select(); } }, 80);
    });
  }

  async function pdfPrepareDestination(suggestedName){
    if(nativeHas('savePdf')) return null;          // the app itself decides where to save
    if(!pdfSaveSupportsPicker()) return null;
    try{
      var handle = await window.showSaveFilePicker({
        suggestedName: pdfEnsureExtension(suggestedName),
        types: [{description: 'PDF document', accept: {'application/pdf': ['.pdf']}}]
      });
      return {handle: handle, name: handle.name || pdfEnsureExtension(suggestedName)};
    }catch(e){
      if(e && e.name === 'AbortError') return false;
      console.warn('Save picker unavailable', e);
      return null;
    }
  }

  // returns 'saved' | 'handed' | false (cancelled)
  async function pdfStoreBlob(blob, filename, destination){
    filename = pdfEnsureExtension(filename);
    if(destination && destination.handle){
      var writable = await destination.handle.createWritable();
      await writable.write(blob); await writable.close();
      try{ var f = await destination.handle.getFile(); if(f && f.size !== blob.size) throw new Error('حجم الملف المحفوظ لا يطابق الملف الأصلي'); }catch(e){ if(e && /حجم/.test(e.message)) throw e; }
      return 'saved';
    }
    if(nativeHas('savePdf')){
      try{
        var out = nativeOutcome(await nativeInvoke('savePdf', await blobToBase64(blob), filename));
        if(out === 'ok') return 'saved';
        if(out === 'cancel') return false;
        console.warn('DaragatNative.savePdf reported an error; falling back to the browser chooser');
      }catch(e){ console.warn('DaragatNative.savePdf failed', e); }
    }
    return await pdfCreateChooser(blob, filename);
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- @page geometry
  var PAGE_SIZES = {a3: [297, 420], a4: [210, 297], a5: [148, 210], b4: [250, 353], b5: [176, 250], letter: [215.9, 279.4], legal: [215.9, 355.6]};
  function lenToMm(tok){
    var m = String(tok || '').trim().toLowerCase().match(/^(-?[\d.]+)(mm|cm|in|px|pt)?$/);
    if(!m) return null;
    var v = parseFloat(m[1]);
    switch(m[2] || 'px'){
      case 'mm': return v;
      case 'cm': return v * 10;
      case 'in': return v * 25.4;
      case 'pt': return v * 25.4 / 72;
      default: return v * MM;
    }
  }
  function readPageRules(doc){
    var size = null, margin = null;
    function scan(rules){
      for(var i = 0; i < rules.length; i++){
        var r = rules[i];
        try{
          if(r.type === 6){
            var t = r.cssText || '';
            var ms = t.match(/(?:^|[;{\s])size\s*:\s*([^;}]+)/i); if(ms) size = ms[1].trim();
            var mm = t.match(/(?:^|[;{\s])margin\s*:\s*([^;}]+)/i); if(mm) margin = mm[1].trim();
          }else if(r.cssRules && r.cssRules.length){
            if(r.type === 4){ var mt = String(r.media && r.media.mediaText || ''); if(/screen/i.test(mt) && !/print/i.test(mt)) continue; }
            scan(r.cssRules);
          }
        }catch(e){}
      }
    }
    try{
      var sheets = doc.styleSheets;
      for(var i = 0; i < sheets.length; i++){ try{ scan(sheets[i].cssRules); }catch(e){} }
    }catch(e){}
    return {size: size, margin: margin};
  }
  function parsePageSize(str){
    if(!str) return null;
    var toks = String(str).toLowerCase().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean), dims = [], land = false, port = false, base = null;
    toks.forEach(function(t){
      if(t === 'landscape') land = true;
      else if(t === 'portrait') port = true;
      else if(PAGE_SIZES[t]) base = PAGE_SIZES[t].slice();
      else { var v = lenToMm(t); if(v != null) dims.push(v); }
    });
    var wh = null;
    if(dims.length >= 2) wh = [dims[0], dims[1]];
    else if(dims.length === 1) wh = [dims[0], dims[0]];
    else if(base) wh = base;
    else if(land || port) wh = PAGE_SIZES.a4.slice();
    if(!wh) return null;
    if(!dims.length){
      if(land && wh[0] < wh[1]) wh = [wh[1], wh[0]];
      if(port && wh[0] > wh[1]) wh = [wh[1], wh[0]];
    }
    return wh;
  }
  function parsePageMargin(str){
    if(!str) return [0, 0, 0, 0];
    var v = String(str).trim().split(/\s+/).map(function(t){ var x = lenToMm(t); return x == null ? 0 : x; });
    if(v.length === 1) return [v[0], v[0], v[0], v[0]];
    if(v.length === 2) return [v[0], v[1], v[0], v[1]];
    if(v.length === 3) return [v[0], v[1], v[2], v[1]];
    return [v[0], v[1], v[2], v[3]];
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- snapshot of a print frame
  function cssLenToPx(str){
    var v = lenToMm(str);
    return v == null ? 0 : v * PX;
  }
  function frameSnapshot(frame){
    var doc = frame.contentDocument;
    var rect = {width: 0, height: 0};
    try{ rect = frame.getBoundingClientRect(); }catch(e){}
    var w = rect.width > 5 ? rect.width : cssLenToPx(frame.style.width) || 794;
    var h = rect.height > 5 ? rect.height : cssLenToPx(frame.style.height) || 1123;
    return {html: '<!DOCTYPE html>' + doc.documentElement.outerHTML, widthPx: w, heightPx: h, title: (doc.title || '')};
  }
  function makeRenderFrame(html, wPx, hPx){
    var f = document.createElement('iframe');
    f.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    f.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;left:-20000px;top:0;width:' + wPx + 'px;height:' + hPx + 'px;border:0;background:#fff';
    document.body.appendChild(f);
    var d = f.contentDocument || f.contentWindow.document;
    d.open(); d.write(html); d.close();
    return f;
  }
  function frameReady(f){
    return new Promise(function(resolve){
      var doc = f.contentDocument, done = false;
      function fin(){ if(!done){ done = true; resolve(); } }
      setTimeout(fin, 3500);
      try{
        void doc.body.offsetHeight;
        var waits = [];
        if(doc.fonts && doc.fonts.ready) waits.push(doc.fonts.ready);
        Array.prototype.slice.call(doc.images || []).forEach(function(img){
          if(!img.complete) waits.push(new Promise(function(r){ img.addEventListener('load', r); img.addEventListener('error', r); }));
        });
        Promise.all(waits).then(function(){ setTimeout(fin, 60); }, fin);
      }catch(e){ fin(); }
    });
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- style helpers
  function cssColor(value){
    value = String(value || '').trim();
    if(!value || value === 'transparent' || value === 'none') return null;
    var m = value.match(/rgba?\(\s*([\d.]+)[, ]+\s*([\d.]+)[, ]+\s*([\d.]+)(?:[, /]+\s*([\d.]+%?))?\s*\)/i);
    if(m){
      var a = m[4] == null ? 1 : (String(m[4]).indexOf('%') > 0 ? parseFloat(m[4]) / 100 : Number(m[4]));
      if(a < 0.08) return null;
      return [Math.round(+m[1]), Math.round(+m[2]), Math.round(+m[3]), a];
    }
    m = value.match(/^#([0-9a-f]{3,8})$/i);
    if(m){
      var h = m[1]; if(h.length === 3) h = h.split('').map(function(x){ return x + x; }).join('');
      return [parseInt(h.slice(0, 2), 16), parseInt(h.slice(2, 4), 16), parseInt(h.slice(4, 6), 16), 1];
    }
    var named = {white: [255, 255, 255, 1], black: [0, 0, 0, 1], red: [255, 0, 0, 1], blue: [0, 0, 255, 1], green: [0, 128, 0, 1], yellow: [255, 255, 0, 1], gray: [128, 128, 128, 1], grey: [128, 128, 128, 1]};
    return named[value.toLowerCase()] || null;
  }
  function gradientColor(bgImage){
    // average of the colour stops inside linear/radial-gradient(...)
    var cols = String(bgImage || '').match(/rgba?\([^)]*\)|#[0-9a-f]{3,8}\b/gi);
    if(!cols || !cols.length) return null;
    var sum = [0, 0, 0], n = 0;
    cols.forEach(function(c){ var p = cssColor(c); if(p){ sum[0] += p[0]; sum[1] += p[1]; sum[2] += p[2]; n++; } });
    return n ? [Math.round(sum[0] / n), Math.round(sum[1] / n), Math.round(sum[2] / n), 1] : null;
  }
  function parseMatrix(t){
    t = String(t || '');
    var m = t.match(/^matrix\(([^)]+)\)$/), p;
    if(m){ p = m[1].split(',').map(parseFloat); return {a: p[0], b: p[1], c: p[2], d: p[3]}; }
    m = t.match(/^matrix3d\(([^)]+)\)$/);
    if(m){ p = m[1].split(',').map(parseFloat); return {a: p[0], b: p[1], c: p[4], d: p[5]}; }
    return null;
  }
  function pickFont(st){
    var fam = String(st.fontFamily || '').toLowerCase().split(',')[0];
    var w = parseInt(st.fontWeight, 10); if(isNaN(w)) w = /bold/i.test(st.fontWeight) ? 700 : 400;
    if(fam.indexOf('kufi') >= 0) return 'DaragatKufi';
    return w >= 600 ? 'DaragatSansB' : 'DaragatSans';
  }
  var METRICS = {}, metricCtx = null;
  function fontDescent(st, fontPx){
    var key = st.fontStyle + ' ' + st.fontWeight + ' ' + st.fontSize + ' ' + st.fontFamily;
    if(METRICS[key] != null) return METRICS[key];
    var d = fontPx * 0.22;
    try{
      if(!metricCtx) metricCtx = document.createElement('canvas').getContext('2d');
      metricCtx.font = key;
      var m = metricCtx.measureText('ابgH');
      if(m && typeof m.fontBoundingBoxDescent === 'number' && m.fontBoundingBoxDescent > 0) d = m.fontBoundingBoxDescent;
    }catch(e){}
    METRICS[key] = d;
    return d;
  }
  function radiusPx(st, w, h){
    var keys = ['borderTopLeftRadius', 'borderTopRightRadius', 'borderBottomRightRadius', 'borderBottomLeftRadius'];
    var r = keys.map(function(k){
      var s = String(st[k] || '0').split(' ')[0], v = parseFloat(s) || 0;
      if(s.indexOf('%') > 0) v = v * Math.min(w, h) / 100;
      return Math.max(0, Math.min(v, Math.min(w, h) / 2));
    });
    var same = r.every(function(x){ return Math.abs(x - r[0]) < 0.6; });
    return same ? r[0] : 0;
  }
  function hashStr(s){
    var h = 5381, n = s.length, step = Math.max(1, Math.floor(n / 4000));
    for(var i = 0; i < n; i += step) h = ((h << 5) + h + s.charCodeAt(i)) | 0;
    return 'i' + (h >>> 0).toString(36) + n.toString(36);
  }


  function splitTop(s){
    var out = [], depth = 0, cur = '';
    for(var i = 0; i < s.length; i++){
      var ch = s.charAt(i);
      if(ch === '(') depth++; else if(ch === ')') depth--;
      if(ch === ',' && depth === 0){ out.push(cur.trim()); cur = ''; } else cur += ch;
    }
    if(cur.trim()) out.push(cur.trim());
    return out;
  }
  function parseGradient(bg){
    var m = String(bg || '').match(/^linear-gradient\((.*)\)/i);
    if(!m) return null;
    var parts = splitTop(m[1]), angle = 180, first = parts[0].toLowerCase();
    if(/^to\s/.test(first)){
      var t = first.replace(/^to\s+/, '');
      angle = (t === 'right') ? 90 : (t === 'left') ? 270 : (t === 'top') ? 0 : (t === 'bottom') ? 180 : (/right/.test(t) ? (/top/.test(t) ? 45 : 135) : (/top/.test(t) ? 315 : 225));
      parts.shift();
    }else if(/^-?[\d.]+(deg|turn|rad|grad)$/.test(first)){
      var v = parseFloat(first);
      angle = /turn/.test(first) ? v * 360 : (/rad/.test(first) ? v * 180 / Math.PI : (/grad/.test(first) ? v * 0.9 : v));
      parts.shift();
    }
    var stops = [];
    parts.forEach(function(p){
      var cm = p.match(/^(rgba?\([^)]*\)|#[0-9a-f]{3,8}|[a-z]+)\s*(-?[\d.]+%)?/i);
      if(!cm) return;
      var c = cssColor(cm[1]); if(!c) c = (cm[1] === 'transparent') ? null : null;
      if(!c) return;
      stops.push({c: c, p: cm[2] ? parseFloat(cm[2]) / 100 : null});
    });
    if(stops.length < 2) return null;
    if(stops[0].p == null) stops[0].p = 0;
    if(stops[stops.length - 1].p == null) stops[stops.length - 1].p = 1;
    var i = 0;
    while(i < stops.length){
      if(stops[i].p != null){ i++; continue; }
      var j = i; while(j < stops.length && stops[j].p == null) j++;
      var a = stops[i - 1].p, bb = stops[j].p;
      for(var k = i; k < j; k++) stops[k].p = a + (bb - a) * (k - i + 1) / (j - i + 1);
      i = j;
    }
    return {angle: ((angle % 360) + 360) % 360, stops: stops};
  }
  function parseClipPolygon(cp, w, h){
    var m = String(cp || '').match(/^polygon\((.*)\)$/i);
    if(!m) return null;
    var pts = splitTop(m[1]).map(function(pair){
      var t = pair.trim().split(/\s+/);
      if(t.length < 2) return null;
      function cv(s, ref){ return s.indexOf('%') > 0 ? ref * parseFloat(s) / 100 : (parseFloat(s) || 0); }
      return [cv(t[0], w), cv(t[1], h)];
    });
    if(pts.some(function(p){ return !p; }) || pts.length < 3) return null;
    return pts;
  }
  // very small flat graphics (e.g. QR codes) look blurry when a viewer smooths them: enlarge with nearest-neighbour
  function crispUpscale(source, nw, nh){
    try{
      if(!nw || !nh || nw > 300 || nh > 300) return null;
      var c0 = document.createElement('canvas'); c0.width = nw; c0.height = nh;
      var x0 = c0.getContext('2d'); x0.drawImage(source, 0, 0);
      var d = x0.getImageData(0, 0, nw, nh).data, seen = {}, n = 0;
      for(var i = 0; i < d.length; i += 4){
        var key = d[i] + ',' + d[i + 1] + ',' + d[i + 2] + ',' + d[i + 3];
        if(!seen[key]){ seen[key] = 1; n++; if(n > 8) return null; }
      }
      var k = Math.min(10, Math.max(2, Math.ceil(900 / Math.max(nw, nh))));
      var c1 = document.createElement('canvas'); c1.width = nw * k; c1.height = nh * k;
      var x1 = c1.getContext('2d'); x1.imageSmoothingEnabled = false; x1.drawImage(c0, 0, 0, nw * k, nh * k);
      return c1.toDataURL('image/png');
    }catch(e){ return null; }
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- collect the drawing model of one page-like target
  // Everything is stored in CSS px relative to the top-left corner of the target.
  function newCtx(z){ return {z: z || 0, boxes: [], images: [], texts: [], children: []}; }

  function textLines(node, st, fontPx){
    var text = String(node.nodeValue || ''), owner = node.ownerDocument, range = owner.createRange();
    var lines = [], cur = null, desc = fontDescent(st, fontPx);
    for(var i = 0; i < text.length; i++){
      var ch = text.charAt(i), blank = /\s/.test(ch);
      range.setStart(node, i); range.setEnd(node, i + 1);
      var rs = range.getClientRects();
      if(!rs.length) continue;
      var r = rs[0];
      if(r.width === 0 && r.height === 0) continue;
      var mid = (r.top + r.bottom) / 2;
      if(blank){ if(cur && Math.abs(mid - cur.mid) <= Math.max(4, fontPx * 0.8)) cur.text += ch; continue; }
      if(!cur || Math.abs(mid - cur.mid) > Math.max(4, fontPx * 0.8)){
        cur = {mid: mid, top: r.top, bottom: r.bottom, left: r.left, right: r.right, base: r.bottom - desc, text: ''};
        lines.push(cur);
      }
      cur.text += ch;
      if(r.left < cur.left) cur.left = r.left;
      if(r.right > cur.right) cur.right = r.right;
      if(r.top < cur.top) cur.top = r.top;
      if(r.bottom > cur.bottom) cur.bottom = r.bottom;
    }
    return lines;
  }

  async function collectTarget(target, win){
    var base = target.getBoundingClientRect();
    var root = newCtx(0), pending = [], breaks = [];
    function rel(r){ return {x: r.left - base.left, y: r.top - base.top, w: r.width, h: r.height}; }

    function imageClip(el, st){
      // circular / rounded clip coming from the image itself or a nearby overflow:hidden ancestor
      var n = el, depth = 0;
      while(n && n !== target.parentElement && depth < 4){
        var s = win.getComputedStyle(n);
        var own = n === el;
        if(own || (s.overflow && s.overflow !== 'visible') || (s.overflowX && s.overflowX !== 'visible')){
          var rr = n.getBoundingClientRect(), rad = radiusPx(s, rr.width, rr.height);
          if(rad > 0.5){
            var q = rel(rr);
            return {x: q.x, y: q.y, w: q.w, h: q.h, r: rad, ellipse: rad >= Math.min(rr.width, rr.height) / 2 - 0.6};
          }
        }
        n = n.parentElement; depth++;
      }
      return null;
    }

    function addBoxRec(ctx, el, st, r, angle, op, pseudo){
      var q = rel(r);
      if(q.w <= 0 || q.h <= 0) return;
      var bg = cssColor(st.backgroundColor);
      if(!bg && st.backgroundImage && /gradient/i.test(st.backgroundImage)) bg = gradientColor(st.backgroundImage);
      var sides = ['Top', 'Right', 'Bottom', 'Left'].map(function(sd){
        var w = parseFloat(st['border' + sd + 'Width'] || '0') || 0, s = st['border' + sd + 'Style'] || 'none';
        var c = cssColor(st['border' + sd + 'Color']);
        return (w > 0 && s !== 'none' && s !== 'hidden' && c) ? {w: w, style: s, color: c} : null;
      });
      var bgUrl = null;
      var mu = String(st.backgroundImage || '').match(/url\((["']?)(data:image\/[^"')]+)\1\)/i);
      if(mu) bgUrl = mu[2];
      if(!bg && !bgUrl && !sides.some(Boolean)) return;
      var rec = {x: q.x, y: q.y, w: q.w, h: q.h, bg: bg, sides: sides, radius: radiusPx(st, q.w, q.h), angle: angle, op: op};
      if(!angle && st.backgroundImage && /linear-gradient/i.test(st.backgroundImage)) rec.grad = parseGradient(st.backgroundImage);
      if(!angle && st.clipPath && /polygon/i.test(st.clipPath)) rec.poly = parseClipPolygon(st.clipPath, q.w, q.h);
      if(angle){ rec.ow = pseudo ? q.w : (el.offsetWidth || q.w); rec.oh = pseudo ? q.h : (el.offsetHeight || q.h); }
      ctx.boxes.push(rec);
      if(bgUrl && !angle){
        var img = {kind: 'bg', src: bgUrl, x: q.x, y: q.y, w: q.w, h: q.h, size: st.backgroundSize, pos: st.backgroundPosition, op: op, clip: {x: q.x, y: q.y, w: q.w, h: q.h, r: rec.radius, ellipse: false}};
        ctx.images.push(img); pending.push(img);
      }
    }
    function addPseudo(ctx, el, which, angle, op){
      try{
        var ps = win.getComputedStyle(el, which);
        if(!ps || ps.content === 'none' || ps.content === 'normal' || ps.display === 'none') return;
        if(ps.position !== 'absolute') return;
        var l = parseFloat(ps.left), t = parseFloat(ps.top), w = parseFloat(ps.width), h = parseFloat(ps.height);
        if(isNaN(l) || isNaN(t) || isNaN(w) || isNaN(h) || w <= 0 || h <= 0) return;
        var pr = el.getBoundingClientRect(), pst = win.getComputedStyle(el);
        var bl = parseFloat(pst.borderLeftWidth) || 0, bt = parseFloat(pst.borderTopWidth) || 0;
        var bw = parseFloat(ps.borderLeftWidth) || 0, bh = parseFloat(ps.borderTopWidth) || 0;
        var pw = parseFloat(ps.paddingLeft) + parseFloat(ps.paddingRight) || 0, ph = parseFloat(ps.paddingTop) + parseFloat(ps.paddingBottom) || 0;
        var fw = w, fh = h;
        if(ps.boxSizing !== 'border-box'){ fw = w + pw + 2 * bw; fh = h + ph + 2 * bh; }
        addBoxRec(ctx, el, ps, {left: pr.left + bl + l, top: pr.top + bt + t, width: fw, height: fh}, 0, op, true);
      }catch(e){}
    }

    function walk(el, ctx, angle, op){
      var tag = String(el.tagName || '').toLowerCase();
      if(/^(script|style|link|meta|head|title|noscript|template|option|datalist)$/.test(tag)) return;
      var st = win.getComputedStyle(el);
      if(st.display === 'none') return;
      var eop = op * (isNaN(parseFloat(st.opacity)) ? 1 : parseFloat(st.opacity));
      if(eop < 0.05) return;
      var m = parseMatrix(st.transform), a2 = angle;
      if(m){ var ang = Math.atan2(m.b, m.a); if(Math.abs(ang) > 0.005) a2 = angle + ang; }
      var z = parseInt(st.zIndex, 10);
      var makesCtx = el !== target && ((st.position && st.position !== 'static') || !!m);
      var cctx = ctx;
      if(makesCtx){ cctx = newCtx(isNaN(z) ? 0 : z); ctx.children.push(cctx); }
      var r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      var visible = st.visibility !== 'hidden' && r.width > 0 && r.height > 0;

      if(visible){
        if(tag === 'tr' || tag === 'li'){ breaks.push(r.bottom - base.top); }
        if(el.parentElement === target || (el.parentElement && el.parentElement.parentElement === target)) breaks.push(r.bottom - base.top);
        addBoxRec(cctx, el, st, r, a2, eop, false);
        if(tag === 'img' || tag === 'canvas'){
          var q = rel(r), rec = {kind: tag, el: el, x: q.x, y: q.y, w: q.w, h: q.h, op: eop, angle: a2, fit: st.objectFit, clip: imageClip(el, st)};
          cctx.images.push(rec); pending.push(rec);
        }
        addPseudo(cctx, el, '::before', a2, eop);
      }
      if(tag === 'img' || tag === 'canvas' || tag === 'svg' || tag === 'select' || tag === 'textarea') { /* leaf-ish */ }
      if(tag === 'input'){
        var v = el.value; if(v && st.visibility !== 'hidden'){ /* inputs are rendered as text */ addInputText(cctx, el, st, r, eop, a2); }
        return;
      }
      var kids = el.childNodes;
      for(var i = 0; i < kids.length; i++){
        var k = kids[i];
        if(k.nodeType === 1) walk(k, cctx, a2, eop);
        else if(k.nodeType === 3 && st.visibility !== 'hidden' && k.nodeValue && k.nodeValue.trim()) addText(cctx, k, el, st, a2, eop);
      }
      if(visible) addPseudo(cctx, el, '::after', a2, eop);
    }

    function baseText(st){
      var fontPx = parseFloat(st.fontSize) || 12;
      var color = cssColor(st.color) || [0, 0, 0, 1];
      var align = st.textAlign || '', dir = st.direction || 'rtl';
      var anchor;
      if(align === 'center') anchor = 'center';
      else if(align === 'right') anchor = 'right';
      else if(align === 'left') anchor = 'left';
      else if(align === 'end') anchor = dir === 'rtl' ? 'left' : 'right';
      else anchor = dir === 'rtl' ? 'right' : 'left';
      return {fontPx: fontPx, color: color, anchor: anchor, dir: dir, font: pickFont(st), underline: /underline/.test(st.textDecorationLine || st.textDecoration || '')};
    }
    function addText(ctx, node, el, st, angle, op){
      var b = baseText(st), rec = {fontPx: b.fontPx, color: b.color, anchor: b.anchor, dir: b.dir, font: b.font, underline: b.underline, op: op, angle: 0, lines: []};
      if(angle){
        // rotated text (e.g. corner ribbons): keep it as one rotated string centred on its box
        var rg = node.ownerDocument.createRange(); rg.selectNodeContents(node);
        var rs = rg.getClientRects(); if(!rs.length) return;
        var L = 1e9, T = 1e9, R = -1e9, B = -1e9;
        for(var i = 0; i < rs.length; i++){ L = Math.min(L, rs[i].left); T = Math.min(T, rs[i].top); R = Math.max(R, rs[i].right); B = Math.max(B, rs[i].bottom); }
        var asc = b.fontPx * 0.95, desc = fontDescent(st, b.fontPx);
        rec.angle = angle;
        rec.rot = {cx: (L + R) / 2 - base.left, cy: (T + B) / 2 - base.top, text: String(node.nodeValue).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(), maxW: (el.offsetWidth || (R - L)), asc: asc, desc: desc};
        ctx.texts.push(rec);
        return;
      }
      var lines = textLines(node, st, b.fontPx);
      lines.forEach(function(l){
        rec.lines.push({text: l.text, left: l.left - base.left, right: l.right - base.left, top: l.top - base.top, bottom: l.bottom - base.top, base: l.base - base.top});
      });
      if(rec.lines.length) ctx.texts.push(rec);
    }
    function addInputText(ctx, el, st, r, op, angle){
      if(angle) return;
      var b = baseText(st), fontPx = b.fontPx, desc = fontDescent(st, fontPx);
      var pl = parseFloat(st.paddingLeft) || 0, pr = parseFloat(st.paddingRight) || 0;
      var text = String(el.value || '');
      var left = r.left + pl, right = r.right - pr;
      var cy = (r.top + r.bottom) / 2;
      var line = {text: text, left: left - base.left, right: right - base.left, top: cy - fontPx * 0.6 - base.top, bottom: cy + fontPx * 0.6 - base.top, base: cy + fontPx * 0.35 - base.top};
      if(b.anchor === 'center') { /* keep */ }
      ctx.texts.push({fontPx: fontPx, color: b.color, anchor: b.anchor, dir: b.dir, font: b.font, underline: false, op: op, angle: 0, lines: [line], fixedBox: true});
    }

    walk(target, root, 0, 1);

    // load / convert images (also gives the natural size used by object-fit and background-size)
    await Promise.all(pending.map(function(rec){
      return new Promise(function(resolve){
        try{
          if(rec.kind === 'canvas'){
            try{ rec.src = rec.el.toDataURL('image/png'); }catch(e){ rec.src = null; }
            rec.nw = rec.el.width; rec.nh = rec.el.height;
            if(rec.src){ var up0 = crispUpscale(rec.el, rec.nw, rec.nh); if(up0) rec.src = up0; }
            resolve(); return;
          }
          if(rec.kind === 'img'){
            var el = rec.el, src = el.currentSrc || el.src || '';
            rec.nw = el.naturalWidth || 0; rec.nh = el.naturalHeight || 0;
            if(/^data:image\/(png|jpeg|jpg);/i.test(src)){
              rec.src = src;
              if(/^data:image\/png/i.test(src) && el.complete){ var up1 = crispUpscale(el, rec.nw, rec.nh); if(up1) rec.src = up1; }
              resolve(); return;
            }
            if(el.complete && el.naturalWidth){
              try{
                var c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = el.naturalWidth; c.height = el.naturalHeight;
                c.getContext('2d').drawImage(el, 0, 0);
                rec.src = c.toDataURL('image/png');
              }catch(e){ rec.src = null; }
            }
            resolve(); return;
          }
          // background image (data URL)
          var im = new Image();
          im.onload = function(){ rec.nw = im.naturalWidth; rec.nh = im.naturalHeight; if(!/^data:image\/(png|jpeg|jpg);/i.test(rec.src)){ try{ var c2 = document.createElement('canvas'); c2.width = im.naturalWidth; c2.height = im.naturalHeight; c2.getContext('2d').drawImage(im, 0, 0); rec.src = c2.toDataURL('image/png'); }catch(e){ rec.src = null; } } resolve(); };
          im.onerror = function(){ rec.src = null; resolve(); };
          im.src = rec.src;
          setTimeout(resolve, 2500);
        }catch(e){ rec.src = null; resolve(); }
      });
    }));
    // fix kind for img elements whose data URL was a non-tagged format
    return {root: root, base: base, breaks: breaks, width: Math.max(1, target.scrollWidth || base.width), height: Math.max(1, target.scrollHeight || base.height)};
  }
  function pageTargets(doc){
    var body = doc && doc.body; if(!body) return [];
    function vis(el){
      return Array.prototype.slice.call(el.children || []).filter(function(x){
        if(/^(script|style|link|meta)$/i.test(x.tagName)) return false;
        var r = x.getBoundingClientRect(); return r.width > 0 && r.height > 0;
      });
    }
    function pageish(x){ return /page|paper|sheet/i.test(String(x.className || '') + ' ' + (x.id || '')); }
    var direct = vis(body);
    if(direct.length === 1){
      var root = direct[0], id = root.id || '', children = vis(root);
      if(id === 'pagesScale'){
        var p = root.querySelector('#pages');
        if(p){ var pc = vis(p); if(pc.length) return pc; }
      }
      if(id === 'pages' || id === 'examPrintHost' || /printHost/i.test(id)){ if(children.length) return children; }
      if(children.length > 1 && children.every(pageish)) return children;
      return [root];
    }
    if(direct.length && direct.every(pageish)) return direct;
    return [body];
  }
  function makeSlices(H, pageH, cands){
    if(H <= pageH + 1) return [[0, H]];
    var out = [], y0 = 0, guard = 0;
    cands = cands.slice().sort(function(a, b){ return a - b; });
    while(y0 < H - 1 && guard++ < 400){
      var limit = y0 + pageH;
      if(limit >= H - 1){ out.push([y0, H]); break; }
      var best = -1;
      for(var i = 0; i < cands.length; i++){ var c = cands[i]; if(c > y0 + pageH * 0.3 && c <= limit + 0.5) best = c; }
      var y1 = best > 0 ? best : limit;
      out.push([y0, y1]); y0 = y1;
    }
    return out;
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- drawing
  function setOp(S, v){
    v = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v));
    if(Math.abs((S.curOp || 1) - v) < 0.01) return;
    try{ S.pdf.setGState(new S.pdf.GState({opacity: v, 'stroke-opacity': v})); S.curOp = v; }catch(e){}
  }
  function withClip(S, shape, fn){
    var pdf = S.pdf, clipped = false;
    if(shape){
      try{
        pdf.saveGraphicsState(); clipped = true;
        if(shape.pts){
          var P = shape.pts, segs = [];
          for(var pi = 1; pi < P.length; pi++) segs.push([P[pi][0] - P[pi - 1][0], P[pi][1] - P[pi - 1][1]]);
          pdf.lines(segs, P[0][0], P[0][1], [1, 1], null, true);
        }
        else if(shape.ellipse) pdf.ellipse(shape.x + shape.w / 2, shape.y + shape.h / 2, shape.w / 2, shape.h / 2, null);
        else if(shape.r > 0) pdf.roundedRect(shape.x, shape.y, shape.w, shape.h, shape.r, shape.r, null);
        else pdf.rect(shape.x, shape.y, shape.w, shape.h, null);
        pdf.clip(); pdf.discardPath();
      }catch(e){ if(clipped){ try{ pdf.restoreGraphicsState(); }catch(x){} } clipped = false; }
    }
    try{ fn(); }
    finally{ if(clipped){ try{ pdf.restoreGraphicsState(); S.curOp = 1; }catch(e){} } }
  }
  function sameSide(a, b){ return a && b && a.w === b.w && a.style === b.style && a.color[0] === b.color[0] && a.color[1] === b.color[1] && a.color[2] === b.color[2]; }
  function setDash(pdf, style, lw){
    try{
      if(style === 'dashed') pdf.setLineDashPattern([lw * 3, lw * 2], 0);
      else if(style === 'dotted') pdf.setLineDashPattern([lw * 0.9, lw * 1.6], 0);
    }catch(e){}
  }
  function clearDash(pdf){ try{ pdf.setLineDashPattern([], 0); }catch(e){} }


  function gradColorAt(stops, t){
    if(t <= stops[0].p) return stops[0].c;
    for(var i = 1; i < stops.length; i++){
      if(t <= stops[i].p){
        var a = stops[i - 1], b = stops[i], f = (b.p === a.p) ? 1 : (t - a.p) / (b.p - a.p);
        return [Math.round(a.c[0] + (b.c[0] - a.c[0]) * f), Math.round(a.c[1] + (b.c[1] - a.c[1]) * f), Math.round(a.c[2] + (b.c[2] - a.c[2]) * f)];
      }
    }
    return stops[stops.length - 1].c;
  }
  function drawGradient(pdf, S, g, x, y, w, h, op){
    var rad = g.angle * Math.PI / 180, sx = Math.sin(rad), cy = -Math.cos(rad);
    var horizontal = Math.abs(sx) >= Math.abs(cy), rev = horizontal ? sx < 0 : cy < 0;
    var n = Math.max(8, Math.min(60, Math.round((horizontal ? w : h) / 2.2)));
    for(var i = 0; i < n; i++){
      var t = (i + 0.5) / n; if(rev) t = 1 - t;
      var c = gradColorAt(g.stops, t);
      pdf.setFillColor(c[0], c[1], c[2]);
      var f0 = i / n, f1 = (i + 1) / n, ov = 0.12;
      if(horizontal) pdf.rect(x + f0 * w, y, (f1 - f0) * w + (i < n - 1 ? ov : 0), h, 'F');
      else pdf.rect(x, y + f0 * h, w, (f1 - f0) * h + (i < n - 1 ? ov : 0), 'F');
    }
  }

  function drawBox(b, S){
    if(b.y + b.h < S.y0 - 0.5 || b.y > S.y1 + 0.5) return;
    var pdf = S.pdf, x = S.X(b.x), y = S.Y(b.y), w = b.w * MM, h = b.h * MM;
    if(b.angle){
      if(!b.bg) return;
      var a = b.angle, ca = Math.cos(a), sa = Math.sin(a), cx = x + w / 2, cy = y + h / 2, hw = b.ow * MM / 2, hh = b.oh * MM / 2;
      var pts = [[-hw, -hh], [hw, -hh], [hw, hh], [-hw, hh]].map(function(p){ return [cx + p[0] * ca - p[1] * sa, cy + p[0] * sa + p[1] * ca]; });
      setOp(S, b.op * b.bg[3]);
      pdf.setFillColor(b.bg[0], b.bg[1], b.bg[2]);
      pdf.lines([[pts[1][0] - pts[0][0], pts[1][1] - pts[0][1]], [pts[2][0] - pts[1][0], pts[2][1] - pts[1][1]], [pts[3][0] - pts[2][0], pts[3][1] - pts[2][1]]], pts[0][0], pts[0][1], [1, 1], 'F', true);
      return;
    }
    var rx = b.radius * MM;
    var pts = b.poly ? b.poly.map(function(p){ return [x + p[0] * MM, y + p[1] * MM]; }) : null;
    var shape = pts ? {pts: pts} : (rx > 0.05 ? {x: x, y: y, w: w, h: h, r: rx, ellipse: false} : null);
    if(b.grad && (b.bg || true)){
      setOp(S, b.op);
      withClip(S, shape, function(){ drawGradient(pdf, S, b.grad, x, y, w, h, b.op); });
    }else if(b.bg){
      setOp(S, b.op * b.bg[3]);
      pdf.setFillColor(b.bg[0], b.bg[1], b.bg[2]);
      if(pts){
        var segs = []; for(var pi = 1; pi < pts.length; pi++) segs.push([pts[pi][0] - pts[pi - 1][0], pts[pi][1] - pts[pi - 1][1]]);
        pdf.lines(segs, pts[0][0], pts[0][1], [1, 1], 'F', true);
      }else if(rx > 0.05) pdf.roundedRect(x, y, w, h, rx, rx, 'F'); else pdf.rect(x, y, w, h, 'F');
    }
    if(pts) return;
    var s = b.sides, s0 = s[0];
    if(s0 && sameSide(s0, s[1]) && sameSide(s0, s[2]) && sameSide(s0, s[3])){
      var lw = s0.w * MM;
      setOp(S, b.op * s0.color[3]);
      pdf.setDrawColor(s0.color[0], s0.color[1], s0.color[2]);
      if(s0.style === 'double' && s0.w >= 3){
        var t3 = lw / 3;
        pdf.setLineWidth(t3);
        [t3 / 2, lw - t3 / 2].forEach(function(off){
          var rr = Math.max(0, rx - off);
          if(rx > 0.05) pdf.roundedRect(x + off, y + off, w - 2 * off, h - 2 * off, rr, rr, 'S'); else pdf.rect(x + off, y + off, w - 2 * off, h - 2 * off, 'S');
        });
      }else{
        pdf.setLineWidth(lw); setDash(pdf, s0.style, lw);
        if(rx > 0.05) pdf.roundedRect(x + lw / 2, y + lw / 2, w - lw, h - lw, Math.max(0, rx - lw / 2), Math.max(0, rx - lw / 2), 'S');
        else pdf.rect(x + lw / 2, y + lw / 2, w - lw, h - lw, 'S');
        clearDash(pdf);
      }
      return;
    }
    ['Top', 'Right', 'Bottom', 'Left'].forEach(function(name, i){
      var sd = s[i]; if(!sd) return;
      var lw2 = sd.w * MM;
      setOp(S, b.op * sd.color[3]);
      pdf.setDrawColor(sd.color[0], sd.color[1], sd.color[2]); pdf.setLineWidth(lw2); setDash(pdf, sd.style, lw2);
      if(name === 'Top') pdf.line(x, y + lw2 / 2, x + w, y + lw2 / 2);
      else if(name === 'Bottom') pdf.line(x, y + h - lw2 / 2, x + w, y + h - lw2 / 2);
      else if(name === 'Left') pdf.line(x + lw2 / 2, y, x + lw2 / 2, y + h);
      else pdf.line(x + w - lw2 / 2, y, x + w - lw2 / 2, y + h);
      clearDash(pdf);
    });
  }

  function drawImage(im, S){
    if(!im.src) return;
    if(im.y + im.h < S.y0 - 0.5 || im.y > S.y1 + 0.5) return;
    var pdf = S.pdf, bx = S.X(im.x), by = S.Y(im.y), bw = im.w * MM, bh = im.h * MM;
    var dx = bx, dy = by, dw = bw, dh = bh, mustClip = false;
    var nw = im.nw || 0, nh = im.nh || 0;
    if(im.kind === 'bg'){
      if(!nw || !nh) return;
      var sz = String(im.size || 'auto').trim().toLowerCase(), sc;
      if(sz === 'cover'){ sc = Math.max(bw / nw, bh / nh); dw = nw * sc; dh = nh * sc; }
      else if(sz === 'contain'){ sc = Math.min(bw / nw, bh / nh); dw = nw * sc; dh = nh * sc; }
      else if(sz === 'auto' || sz === 'auto auto'){ dw = nw * MM; dh = nh * MM; }
      else{
        var pr = sz.split(/\s+/), pw = pr[0], ph = pr.length > 1 ? pr[1] : 'auto';
        function one(tok, ref){ if(tok === 'auto') return null; if(tok.indexOf('%') > 0) return ref * parseFloat(tok) / 100; var v = parseFloat(tok); return isNaN(v) ? null : v * MM; }
        var ww = one(pw, bw), hh2 = one(ph, bh);
        if(ww == null && hh2 == null){ dw = nw * MM; dh = nh * MM; }
        else if(ww == null){ dh = hh2; dw = hh2 * nw / nh; }
        else if(hh2 == null){ dw = ww; dh = ww * nh / nw; }
        else{ dw = ww; dh = hh2; }
      }
      var pp = String(im.pos || '50% 50%').trim().split(/\s+/), px = pp[0], py = pp.length > 1 ? pp[1] : '50%';
      function place(tok, box, size){ if(tok.indexOf('%') > 0) return (box - size) * parseFloat(tok) / 100; var v = parseFloat(tok); return isNaN(v) ? (box - size) / 2 : v * MM; }
      dx = bx + place(px, bw, dw); dy = by + place(py, bh, dh);
      mustClip = dw > bw + 0.1 || dh > bh + 0.1 || dx < bx - 0.1 || dy < by - 0.1;
    }else if(nw && nh){
      var fit = String(im.fit || 'fill');
      if(fit === 'cover' || fit === 'contain' || fit === 'scale-down' || fit === 'none'){
        var s2 = fit === 'cover' ? Math.max(bw / nw, bh / nh) : (fit === 'contain' ? Math.min(bw / nw, bh / nh) : (fit === 'none' ? MM : Math.min(1 * MM, Math.min(bw / nw, bh / nh))));
        dw = nw * s2; dh = nh * s2; dx = bx + (bw - dw) / 2; dy = by + (bh - dh) / 2;
        mustClip = fit === 'cover' || fit === 'none';
      }
    }
    var clip = null;
    if(im.clip){ clip = {x: S.X(im.clip.x), y: S.Y(im.clip.y), w: im.clip.w * MM, h: im.clip.h * MM, r: im.clip.r * MM, ellipse: im.clip.ellipse}; }
    else if(mustClip){ clip = {x: bx, y: by, w: bw, h: bh, r: 0, ellipse: false}; }
    var fmt = /^data:image\/png/i.test(im.src) ? 'PNG' : 'JPEG';
    setOp(S, im.op);
    withClip(S, clip, function(){ pdf.addImage(im.src, fmt, dx, dy, dw, dh, hashStr(im.src), 'FAST'); });
  }

  function safeWidth(pdf, text){ try{ return pdf.getTextWidth(text); }catch(e){ return 0; } }
  function drawText(t, S){
    var pdf = S.pdf;
    setOp(S, t.op * (t.color[3] == null ? 1 : t.color[3]));
    pdf.setFont(t.font, 'normal');
    pdf.setTextColor(t.color[0], t.color[1], t.color[2]);
    var baseSize = t.fontPx * 0.75;
    if(t.angle && t.rot){
      var r = t.rot;
      if(r.cy < S.y0 || r.cy >= S.y1 || !r.text) return;
      var size = baseSize; pdf.setFontSize(size);
      var w = safeWidth(pdf, r.text), maxW = r.maxW * MM;
      if(w > maxW * 0.98 && maxW > 0){ size = Math.max(baseSize * 0.5, baseSize * maxW * 0.98 / w); pdf.setFontSize(size); w = safeWidth(pdf, r.text); }
      var ca = Math.cos(t.angle), sa = Math.sin(t.angle), cx = S.X(r.cx), cy = S.Y(r.cy), off = ((r.asc - r.desc) / 2) * MM;
      pdf.text(r.text, cx - ca * w / 2 - sa * off, cy - sa * w / 2 + ca * off, {angle: -t.angle * 180 / Math.PI});
      return;
    }
    t.lines.forEach(function(l){
      var cy = (l.top + l.bottom) / 2;
      if(cy < S.y0 || cy >= S.y1) return;
      var text = l.text.replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g, '');
      if(!text) return;
      var size = baseSize; pdf.setFontSize(size);
      var domW = (l.right - l.left) * MM, w = safeWidth(pdf, text);
      if(domW > 0 && w > domW * 1.001){ size = Math.max(baseSize * 0.55, baseSize * domW / w); pdf.setFontSize(size); w = safeWidth(pdf, text); }
      var X = t.anchor === 'center' ? S.X((l.left + l.right) / 2) : (t.anchor === 'right' ? S.X(l.right) : S.X(l.left));
      var Y = S.Y(l.base);
      try{ pdf.text(text, X, Y, {align: t.anchor}); }
      catch(e){ try{ pdf.setFont('helvetica', 'normal'); pdf.text(text, X, Y, {align: t.anchor}); pdf.setFont(t.font, 'normal'); }catch(x){} }
      if(t.underline){
        var x1 = t.anchor === 'center' ? X - w / 2 : (t.anchor === 'right' ? X - w : X);
        pdf.setDrawColor(t.color[0], t.color[1], t.color[2]); pdf.setLineWidth(Math.max(0.1, t.fontPx * MM * 0.06));
        pdf.line(x1, Y + t.fontPx * MM * 0.12, x1 + w, Y + t.fontPx * MM * 0.12);
      }
    });
  }
  function drawCtx(ctx, S){
    ctx.boxes.forEach(function(b){ try{ drawBox(b, S); }catch(e){ console.warn('box', e); } });
    ctx.images.forEach(function(im){ try{ drawImage(im, S); }catch(e){ console.warn('image', e); } });
    ctx.texts.forEach(function(t){ try{ drawText(t, S); }catch(e){ console.warn('text', e); } });
    ctx.children.map(function(c, i){ return {c: c, i: i}; })
      .sort(function(a, b){ return (a.c.z - b.c.z) || (a.i - b.i); })
      .forEach(function(o){ drawCtx(o.c, S); });
  }

  function registerFonts(pdf){
    var ok = {kufi: false, sans: false};
    try{
      pdf.addFileToVFS('DaragatKufi.ttf', String(KUFI_FONT_DATA || '').split(',')[1] || '');
      pdf.addFont('DaragatKufi.ttf', 'DaragatKufi', 'normal'); ok.kufi = true;
    }catch(e){ console.warn('Kufi font', e); }
    try{
      pdf.addFileToVFS('DaragatSans.ttf', SANS_REG); pdf.addFont('DaragatSans.ttf', 'DaragatSans', 'normal');
      pdf.addFileToVFS('DaragatSansB.ttf', SANS_BOLD); pdf.addFont('DaragatSansB.ttf', 'DaragatSansB', 'normal');
      ok.sans = true;
    }catch(e){ console.warn('Sans font', e); }
    if(!ok.sans && ok.kufi){ // fall back to the Kufi face for everything
      try{ pdf.addFont('DaragatKufi.ttf', 'DaragatSans', 'normal'); pdf.addFont('DaragatKufi.ttf', 'DaragatSansB', 'normal'); }catch(e){}
    }
    if(!ok.kufi && ok.sans){
      try{ pdf.addFont('DaragatSans.ttf', 'DaragatKufi', 'normal'); }catch(e){}
    }
    if(!ok.kufi && !ok.sans) throw new Error('تعذر تحميل خطوط PDF');
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- PDF from a print snapshot
  async function buildPdfFromSnapshot(snap, filename){
    var jsp = window.jspdf && window.jspdf.jsPDF;
    if(!jsp) throw new Error('مولّد PDF الحقيقي غير متاح');
    var rf = makeRenderFrame(snap.html, snap.widthPx, snap.heightPx);
    try{
      var doc = rf.contentDocument, win = rf.contentWindow;
      try{ if(typeof applyDigitsToPrintDoc === 'function') applyDigitsToPrintDoc(doc); }catch(e){}
      await sleep(60);
      var rules = readPageRules(doc), size = parsePageSize(rules.size), mar = parsePageMargin(rules.margin);
      var pwMm = size ? size[0] : snap.widthPx * MM, phMm = size ? size[1] : snap.heightPx * MM;
      var innerWpx = Math.max(50, (pwMm - mar[1] - mar[3]) * PX), innerHpx = Math.max(50, (phMm - mar[0] - mar[2]) * PX);
      if(size || mar[0] || mar[1] || mar[2] || mar[3]){
        rf.style.width = innerWpx + 'px'; rf.style.height = innerHpx + 'px';
        await sleep(40);
      }
      await frameReady(rf);
      var pdf = new jsp({unit: 'mm', format: [pwMm, phMm], orientation: pwMm > phMm ? 'landscape' : 'portrait', putOnlyUsedFonts: true, compress: true});
      registerFonts(pdf);
      pdf.setProperties({title: filename || 'Daragat PDF', subject: 'School print export', author: 'Daragat'});
      var targets = pageTargets(doc); if(!targets.length) targets = [doc.body];
      var pageIndex = 0;
      for(var ti = 0; ti < targets.length; ti++){
        var target = targets[ti];
        var model = await collectTarget(target, win);
        var slices = makeSlices(model.height, innerHpx, model.breaks);
        for(var si = 0; si < slices.length; si++){
          if(pageIndex > 0) pdf.addPage([pwMm, phMm], pwMm > phMm ? 'landscape' : 'portrait');
          pageIndex++;
          var topShift = (targets.length === 1 && target !== doc.body && si === 0) ? Math.max(0, model.base.top) * MM : 0;
          var S = {pdf: pdf, y0: slices[si][0], y1: slices[si][1], curOp: 1,
                   ox: mar[3] + model.base.left * MM, oy: mar[0] + topShift};
          S.X = function(x){ return S.ox + x * MM; };
          S.Y = function(y){ return S.oy + (y - S.y0) * MM; };
          var clip = (mar[0] > 0 || mar[2] > 0 || slices.length > 1) ? {x: 0, y: mar[0], w: pwMm, h: phMm - mar[0] - mar[2], r: 0, ellipse: false} : null;
          withClip(S, clip, function(){ drawCtx(model.root, S); });
        }
      }
      if(!pageIndex) throw new Error('لم يتم العثور على محتوى للطباعة');
      return pdf.output('blob');
    }finally{
      try{ rf.remove(); }catch(e){}
    }
  }
